Here are the steps to find the solution:

1. **Identify the black nodes:** R and P.
2. **Trace all possible paths between R and P:**
   - R → C → B → F → E → S → A → N → G → O → Q → I → J → H → P
   - R → C → B → F → T → H → P
   - R → C → B → G → O → Q → I → J → H → P
